│             │
│             │ ### Changes Made
│             │
│             │ **File**: `vllm/worker/neuron_worker.py`
│             │ **Method**: `prepare_worker_input()`
│             │ **Lines changed**: 4 lines (2 insertions, 2 deletions)
│             │
│             │ ### The Problem
│             │
│             │ The `prepare_worker_input()` method in the `NeuronWorker` class
│             │ returned `WorkerInput` object:
│             │ 1. `virtual_engine` - Required for proper engine selection and K
│             │ 2. `num_steps` - Required for determining the number of forward
│             │
│             │ This omission could lead to:
│             │ - Incorrect engine selection in multi-engine scenarios
│             │ - Improper step counting during model execution
│             │ - Performance degradation due to fallback behavior
│             │ - Potential correctness issues
│             │
│             │ ### The Solution
│             │
│             │ Modified the `prepare_worker_input()` method to include all requ
│             │
│             │ **Before:**
│             │ ```python
│             │ @torch.inference_mode()
│             │ def prepare_worker_input(
│             │         self, execute_model_req: ExecuteModelRequest) -> WorkerI
│             │     return WorkerInput(num_seq_groups=len(
│             │         execute_model_req.seq_group_metadata_list), )
│             │ ```
│             │
│             │ **After:**
│             │ ```python
│             │ @torch.inference_mode()
│             │ def prepare_worker_input(
│             │         self, execute_model_req: ExecuteModelRequest) -> WorkerI
│             │     return WorkerInput(
│             │         num_seq_groups=len(execute_model_req.seq_group_metadata_
│             │         virtual_engine=execute_model_req.virtual_engine,
│             │         num_steps=execute_model_req.num_steps,
│             │     )
│             │ ```
│             │
│             │ ### Verification
│             │
│             │ 1. ✅ Code structure validation passed
